```
def _softmax_lse(y, kappa):
    x = _softmax(y)
    b = np.convolve(x,x)
    z = kappa*b
    m = z.max()
    return float(m + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z-m))))
```
But b values maybe ~1/n; kappa large; okay. Need avoid repeated exp allocation? fine.
Gradient:
```
def _softmax_lse_grad(y, kappa):
   x = softmax(y)
   b = conv(x,x)
   z = kappa*b; m=max; w=np.exp(z-m)/sum
   # A_len = conv(w, x[::-1], 'full')[n-1:n-1+n]
   A = 2*kappa * A_len
   S = dot(x,A)
   grad = x*(A - S)
   return grad
```

```
def _score_np(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    s=x.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return inf
    b=np.convolve(x,x)
    return float(2*x.size*np.max(b)/(s*s))
```
Since we often normalize sum1, score=2n maxb.

def _make_starts(n, rng, base_seq=None, num=16):
    starts=[]
    # powers beta from 0.2 to 0.49 step, gamma 0,1
    for beta in [0.2,0.3,0.35,0.4,0.43,0.45,0.47,0.49]:
       for gamma in [0.0,0.5,1.0,2.0]:
          x = power(n,beta,gamma)
          if score finite, append; also reverse
    # exponential/sigmoid
    for r in [-6,-3,-1.5,0? 1.5,3,6]: append exponential
    for mu in [0.2,0.5,0.8], r [2,4,8] append sigmoid
    # random base scaled
    if base: append base, random perturbed base
    # random smooth: y cumulative normal -> monotone; also random Fourier.
```

def _clean_array(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   s=a.sum()
   if s>0: a=a/s
   return a
```

4. `_make_power(x, n, beta, gamma, quad, shift=0.0, reverse=False)`:
```
i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)+1.0
u=(i-1)/max(1,n-1)
base=np.power(i+shift, -beta)
if gamma !=0: base *= np.exp(gamma*u)
if quad !=0: base *= np.exp(quad*(u-0.5)**2)
if reverse: base=base[::-1]
base=np.clip(base,0,0)
s=base.sum(); if s>0 base/=s
return base
```

7. L-BFGS functions. Need handle if `minimize` None. If None, skip.
Top-level globals:
```
_CUR_KAPPA = 0.0
_CACHE = None
```
But `_objective` and `_grad` called by minimize. We'll not cache.

```
def _obj_g(y):
    x = _softmax(y)
    b = np.convolve(x,x)
    z = _CUR_KAPPA * b
    m = z.max()
    val = m + np.log(np.exp(z-m).sum())
    return float(val)

def _obj_grad(y):
    x = _softmax(y)
    b = np.convolve(x,x)
    z = _CUR_KAPPA*b
    m = z.max()
    w = np.exp(z-m)
    den = w.sum()
    if den ==0: return x? no
    w /= den
    n = x.size
    c = np.convolve(w, x[::-1], 'full')
    A = 2*_CUR_KAPPA * c[n-1:n-1+n]
    s = np.dot(x,A)
    return x*(A-s)
```

8. Local search function:
```
def _local_search(x, deadline, rng):
   x = _clean array sum1
   score = _score_normalized(x)
   best=x; best_score=score
   sigma=0.1
   no_improve=0
   while time<deadline:
      cand=best.copy()
      n=cand.size
      # choose perturbation type
      r=rng.random()
      width=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//100), max(4,n//10)))
      start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width))
      if r<0.6:
         cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma,width))
      elif r<0.8:
         cand[start:start+width] += sigma*rng.standard_normal(width)* (best[start:start+width].mean()+1e-6)
      else:
         # swap mass between two blocks
         p=int(rng.integers(0,start)); q=int(rng.integers(start+width,n))? 
      cand=np.clip(cand,0,None); s=cand.sum(); if s>0 cand/=s
      sc=_score_normalized(cand)
      if sc < best_score: update; no_improve=0; sigma*=0.9
      else: no_improve+=1; if no_improve > 50: sigma*=1.2; maybe restart from best with bigger sigma; if no_improve>500: break?
```
Use exact objective. Since gradient local search already refined, we can run many block perturb in remaining time. Also use "active direction": choose k_max from b of best; then reduce coefficients. Simpler: coordinate transfer.

```

def _convolve_full(a,b):
    n=len(a); m=len(b)
    if n*m <= 3_000_000: return np.convolve(a,b)
    size=1
    while size < n+m-1: size <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,size)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,size)

```

11. Explicit random profile generators:
```
def _random_profile(n, rng):
   r=rng.random()
   if r<0.5:
      y=np.cumsum(rng.normal(0,0.5,n))
      x=np.exp(y-np.max(y))
   elif r<0.8:
      # low-freq positive
      f=rng.integers(1,6)
      phases=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi,5)
      amps=rng.uniform(0.1,1,5)
      t=np.arange(n)/(n-1)
      x=1.0
      for m in range(1,6): x += amps[m-1]*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*t+phases[m-1])
      x=np.clip(x,0,None)
   else: x=rng.lognormal(0,1,n)
   x=np.clip(x,0,0); s=x.sum(); if s>0: x/=s
   return x
```
No nested loops? Top-level loop okay.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    global _CUR_KAPPA
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+max(30,budget_s)-8

`_grid_starts` function: It will evaluate with internal score and return top list. But to avoid too many time, use its own deadline.
```
def _grid_starts(n, max_candidates, rng, deadline):
   out=[]
   # coarse combos
   betas=[0.42,0.45,0.47,0.48,0.49,0.5,0.52]
   for beta in betas:
      for gamma in [-2,-1,0,1,2]:
         for quad in [0,1]:
            for shift in [0,1]:
               if time>deadline: return out
               for rev in [False,True]:
                   x=_power_sequence(n,beta,gamma,quad,shift,rev)
                   sc=_score_internal(x)
                   out.append((sc,x))
   # random params
   while len(out)<max_candidates and time<deadline:
       beta=rng.uniform(0.40,0.55)
       gamma=rng.uniform(-3,3)
       quad=rng.uniform(-3,3)
       shift=rng.choice(np.array([0,0.1,0.5,1,2,5]))
       rev=bool(rng.random()<0.5)
       x=...
       out.append(...)
   sort, top 20, return
```
Need no lambda. Use `np.array` for choices? allowed. To avoid repeated list creation, okay.

The number coarse 7*5*2*2*2=280, plus random. If n=2048, 280 conv with FFT maybe 0.01*2? 280*0.02=5.6s. Fine. Random until deadline maybe 120s, up to thousands, but we cap max_candidates maybe 1000. 1000*0.02=20s. OK. Set `max_candidates=400` to be safe.

```

u=np.linspace(0,1,n)
logx = -beta*np.log(i+shift) + gamma*u + quad*(u-0.5)**2 + cubic*(u-0.5)**3
logx -= max; x=exp(logx)
```
For beta=0.5, gamma etc.

```
import numpy as np
import time
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None

_CUR_KAPPA = 1.0

def _softmax(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    y = y - np.max(y)
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        return np.ones_like(y) / y.size
    return e / s
```
If y large, subtract max. If all -inf? no.

```
def _clean(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = x.sum()
    if s > 1e-15:
        x = x / s
    else:
        x = np.ones(x.size)/x.size
    return x
```

```
def _convolve_full(a,b):
    la=len(a); lb=len(b)
    need=la+lb-1
    if la*lb <= 4_000_000:
        return np.convolve(a,b)
    size=1
    while size < need: size <<=1
    # if size too big? memory maybe. max 32768 for 8192. ok.
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(b, size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb, size)
    return c[:need]
```
For `a` and `b` maybe same; fine. Using `la*lb` threshold; for A w length 8191, x length4096: product33M > threshold -> FFT size16384. Good.

```
def _score_internal(x):
    x = _clean(x)
    b = _convolve_full(x, x)
    s = x.sum() # x normalized 1
    if s < 1e-15: return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * x.size * float(np.max(b)) / (s*s))
```
Since x cleaned/normalized sum1. If we normalize inside, score uses normalized (scale invariant). Good.

But if x input already sum1, `_clean` normalizes again. OK. If we want exact scale? scale invariant.

```
def _log_to_x(logx):
    logx = np.asarray(logx,float)
    logx = logx - np.max(logx)
    x = np.exp(logx)
    s=x.sum(); if s>0 x/=s else ones
    return x
```
Use with reverese.

`_power_profile(n,beta,gamma,quad,cubic,shift,reverse)`:
```
i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)+1.0
u=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
# if n==1, u [0]
logx = -beta * np.log(i + max(0.0, shift))
if gamma: logx += gamma*u
if quad: logx += quad*(u-0.5)**2
if cubic: logx += cubic*(u-0.5)**3
if reverse: logx = logx[::-1]
return _log_to_x(logx)
```
If beta=0, log no issue. If shift causes i+0 positive. Good.

`_random_profile(n,rng)`:
```
r = rng.random()
if r < 0.3:
   # smoothed random walk
   logx = np.cumsum(rng.normal(0,0.35,n))
   # optionally reverse
elif r < 0.6:
   # cosine
   u = np.linspace(0,1,n)
   logx = np.zeros(n)
   for m in range(1,6):
       amp = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0) / m
       ph = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       logx += amp * np.cos(2*np.pi*m*u + ph)
else:
   logx = rng.normal(0,1,n)
# add endpoint power? some probability
if rng.random()<0.4:
   beta=rng.uniform(0.35,0.55)
   shift=rng.uniform(0,2)
   logx += -beta*np.log(np.arange(n)+1.0+shift) * rng.uniform(0,1)
if rng.random()<0.5: logx=logx[::-1]
return _log_to_x(logx)
```
No lambdas.

`_upsample(x, n_out)`:
```
if x.size == n_out: return x.copy()
x=np.asarray(x,float).ravel()
if x.size == 1: return np.full(n_out,x[0]/?)
left=np.linspace(0,1,x.size)
right=np.linspace(0,1,n_out)
y=np.interp(right,left,x)
return _clean(y)
```
If x unnormalized, clean normalizes.

def _grid_starts(n, rng, deadline, max_candidates=500):
    out=[]
    betas = (0.40,0.44,0.47,0.48,0.49,0.50,0.52)
    for beta in betas:
       for gamma in (-2.0,-1.0,-0.3,0.0,0.3,1.0,2.0):
          for quad in (-1.0,0.0,1.0):
             for shift in (0.0,1.0):
                if len(out) >= max_candidates or time.monotonic() > deadline: return out
                for rev in (False, True):
                    x=_power_profile(n,beta,gamma,quad,0.0,shift,rev)
                    sc=_score_internal(x)
                    out.append((sc,x))
                # random cubic for same maybe

       beta = float(rng.uniform(0.38,0.55))
       gamma = float(rng.uniform(-3,3))
       quad = float(rng.uniform(-3,3))
       cubic = float(rng.uniform(-2,2))
       shift = float(rng.choice(np.array([0.0,0.1,0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0])))
       rev = bool(rng.random() < 0.5)
       x = _power_profile(n,beta,gamma,quad,cubic,shift,rev)
       sc = _score_internal(x)
       out.append((sc,x))
    out.sort(key=lambda? no lambda. Need sort without lambda? Python sort key must function; lambda prohibited? Rule no lambda functions. For final sort, define top-level `_sort_starts(lst)`:
```
def _sort_starts(lst):
    if not lst: return lst
    idx = np.argsort(np.array([item[0] for item in lst], dtype=float))
    # np.argsort fails if inf/nan? inf okay. 
    return [lst[i] for i in idx]
```
List comprehension okay. No closures? It's a helper.

def _run_lbfgs(x0, deadline):
    global _CUR_KAPPA
    n=x0.size
    if _scipy_minimize is None: return _clean(x0), _score_internal(x0)
    # determine factors
    if n <=1024: factors=(10.0,40.0,120.0)
    elif n <=4096: factors=(15.0,60.0,150.0)
    else: factors=(20.0,80.0)
    maxiter = max(15, 120000 // max(1,n)) # 2048->58,4096->29,8k->18
    current=x0.copy(); current_score=_score_internal(current)
    for factor in factors:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
       _CUR_KAPPA = float(n * factor)
       y0 = np.log(current + 1e-300)
       y0 = y0 - np.mean(y0)
       try:
          res = _scipy_minimize(_obj_g, y0, jac=_obj_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-10, 'gtol': 1e-6, 'maxls': 20})
       except Exception:
          continue
       x_new = _softmax(res.x)
       # Also maybe result if res.x finite
       sc_new=_score_internal(x_new)
       if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < current_score:
           current = x_new; current_score=sc_new
       # If not, maybe keep current. Try y from res? no
    return current, current_score
```

```
def _sort_starts(lst):
    if not lst: return []
    scores=np.empty(len(lst),float)
    for i,item in enumerate(lst):
       s=item[0]
       if np.isfinite(s): scores[i]=float(s)
       else: scores[i]=1e300
    order=np.argsort(scores)
    return [lst[i] for i in order]
```

Let's refine local search function to be efficient.

```
def _local_search(x, deadline, rng):
    best = _clean(np.asarray(x,float))
    cur = best.copy()
    best_score = _score_internal(best)
    cur_score = best_score
    sigma = 0.12
    T = 0.03
    it=0
    stagnation=0
    n=best.size
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       cand = cur.copy()
       r = float(rng.random())
       if r < 0.40:
           width_low = max(2, n//200)
           width_high = max(width_low+1, n//8)
           width = int(rng.integers(width_low, min(n, width_high)))
           start = int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
           mult = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, width))
           cand[start:start+width] *= mult
       elif r < 0.65:
           width_low=max(1,n//500); width_high=max(width_low+1,n//20)
           width=int(rng.integers(width_low,min(n,width_high)))
           start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
           scale = max(1e-6, float(np.mean(cur[start:start+width])))
           cand[start:start+width] += sigma * scale * rng.standard_normal(width)
       elif r < 0.80:
           # transfer mass between two random blocks
           width = int(rng.integers(max(1,n//1000), max(2,n//100)))
           p=int(rng.integers(0,n-width)); q=int(rng.integers(0,n-width))
           if p!=q:
              alpha=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.5))
              a=cand[p:p+width].copy()
              b=cand[q:q+width].copy()
              cand[p:p+width] = (1-alpha)*a + alpha*b
              cand[q:q+width] = (1-alpha)*b + alpha*a
       else:
           # active reduction: need b from cur
           bvec = _convolve_full(cur, cur)
           k = int(np.argmax(bvec))
           lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
           hi = min(n - 1, k)
           if hi > lo:
              idx = np.arange(lo, hi+1)
              weights = cur[idx] * cur[k - idx]
              pos = int(np.argmax(weights))
              p = int(lo + pos)
              q = k - p
              if q < 0 or q >= n: q = p
              if p != q:
                 transfer = cur[p] * float(rng.uniform(0.02,0.15))
                 rdest = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                 cand[p] = max(0.0, cand[p] - transfer)
                 cand[rdest] += transfer
           else:
              # simple coordinate
              p=int(rng.integers(0,n)); q=int(rng.integers(0,n))
              if p!=q:
                 transfer=cur[p]*0.05
                 cand[p]-=transfer; cand[q]+=transfer
       cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, None)
       s = float(cand.sum())
       if s <= 0: 
           cand = cur.copy(); sc = cur_score
       else:
           cand = cand / s
           sc = _score_internal(cand)
       it += 1
       if np.isfinite(sc):
           if sc < cur_score:
               cur = cand; cur_score = sc
               if sc < best_score:
                   best = cur.copy(); best_score = sc
                   T = max(0.001, T*0.98)
                   stagnation = 0
                   if it % 100 == 0 or best_score < 1.6:
                       print(f"local improve {it}: {best_score:.8f}, t left {max(0.0,deadline-time.monotonic()):.0f}s")
           else:
               accept = False
               if T > 0:
                   delta = sc - cur_score
                   if delta < 1.0:
                       accept_prob = np.exp(-delta / T)
                       if rng.random() < accept_prob:
                           accept = True
               if accept:
                   cur = cand; cur_score = sc
               else:
                   stagnation += 1
                   if stagnation % 200 == 0:
                       T = max(0.001, T*0.9)
                   if stagnation > 500 and rng.random() < 0.05:
                       # pull current back toward best
                       mix = float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.6))
                       cur = (1-mix)*cur + mix*best
                       cur = _clean(cur)
                       cur_score = _score_internal(cur)
                   if stagnation > 5000:
                       cur = best.copy(); cur_score = best_score; T = 0.02; stagnation=0
       else:
           cur = best.copy(); cur_score = best_score
       if it % 1000 == 0 and np.isfinite(best_score):
           print(f"local iter {it}, best {best_score:.8f}, t left {max(0.0,deadline-time.monotonic()):.0f}s")
    return best, best_score
```
This is robust but may print a lot if every improvement; okay. Use `it % 100` conditions. For delta <1.0 to avoid exp underflow; if delta large, accept false.

```python
"""
Search for a nonnegative step-function sequence minimizing

    2 * n * max_k (a * a)[k] / (sum(a))^2

where * is linear convolution.  The submitted sequence is generated by a
multi-stage optimization:

1. A bounded grid over a flexible one-sided singularity/exponential profile
   is sampled to obtain strong starting points.
2. Selected profiles are refined by L-BFGS on the logistic-simplex
   parameterization, using a smooth log-sum-exp approximation of the max
   convolution coefficient.
3. A simulated-annealing/local-search stage refines the best exact sequence.
4. If enough time remains, the best profile is upscaled to a longer length and
   refined again.

All internal scoring uses the same mathematical objective as evaluate_sequence.
"""

import time

import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None

_CUR_KAPPA = 1.0
_SHIFTS = np.array([0.0, 0.1, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0], dtype=np.float64)


def _softmax(y):
    """Stable softmax."""
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if y.size == 0:
        return np.ones(0, dtype=np.float64)
    y = y - np.max(y)
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(y.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(y.size)
    return e / s


def _clean(x):
    """Clip to valid nonnegative floats and normalize to sum one."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
        x = x / s
    else:
        x = np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, x.size))
    return x


def _square_convolve(x):
    """Linear convolution x*x, optimized for repeated use."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 1:
        return x * x
    need = 2 * n - 1

    # For moderate sizes direct convolution is faster and exact enough.
    if n * n <= 4_000_000:
        return np.convolve(x, x)

    size = 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1
    fx = np.fft.rfft(x, size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fx * fx, size)
    return out[:need]


def _convolve_full(a, b):
    """General linear convolution a*b, with FFT for large sizes."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    la = a.size
    lb = b.size
    if la == 0 or lb == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    need = la + lb - 1

    if la * lb <= 4_000_000:
        return np.convolve(a, b)

    size = 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, size)
    return out[:need]


def _score_internal(x):
    """Exact objective for the normalized vector x (scale-invariant)."""
    x = _clean(x)
    if x.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    b = _square_convolve(x)
    mb = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(mb):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * x.size * mb / (s * s))


def _log_to_x(logx):
    """Convert unconstrained log-heights to a normalized nonnegative vector."""
    logx = np.asarray(logx, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if logx.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    logx = logx - np.max(logx)
    x = np.exp(logx)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(logx.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(logx.size)
    return x / s


def _power_profile(n, beta, gamma, quad, cubic, shift, reverse):
    """
    Flexible nonnegative profile:

        log x_i = -beta log(i+1+shift)
                    + gamma u
                    + quad (u-0.5)^2
                    + cubic (u-0.5)^3

    optionally reversed.  It normalizes the result to sum one.
    """
    n = int(max(1, n))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0
    if n == 1:
        u = np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        u = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)

    shift = float(max(0.0, shift))
    if beta == 0.0:
        logx = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        logx = -float(beta) * np.log(i + shift)

    if gamma != 0.0:
        logx = logx + float(gamma) * u
    if quad != 0.0:
        logx = logx + float(quad) * ((u - 0.5) ** 2)
    if cubic != 0.0:
        logx = logx + float(cubic) * ((u - 0.5) ** 3)

    if reverse:
        logx = logx[::-1].copy()

    return _log_to_x(logx)


def _random_profile(n, rng):
    """Random nonnegative profile used for diversity."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    r = float(rng.random())

    if r < 0.35:
        # Smooth multiplicative random walk.
        logx = np.cumsum(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, size=n))
        beta = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.25))
        if beta > 0.0:
            i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0
            logx = logx - beta * np.log(i)
    elif r < 0.70:
        # Low-frequency cosine mix on a log scale.
        u = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        logx = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        m = int(rng.integers(3, 8))
        for k in range(1, m + 1):
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.2 / k))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            logx = logx + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * u + ph)
    else:
        # Independent noise plus optional endpoint singularity.
        logx = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n)
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            beta = float(rng.uniform(0.30, 0.55))
            shift = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0))
            i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0
            logx = logx - beta * np.log(i + shift)

    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        logx = logx[::-1].copy()

    return _log_to_x(logx)


def _upsample(x, n_out):
    """Linearly resample a profile to a new length and normalize."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n_out = int(max(1, n_out))
    if x.size == n_out:
        return _clean(x)
    if x.size == 1:
        return np.full(n_out, float(x[0]), dtype=np.float64)

    left = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    right = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_out, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.interp(right, left, x)
    return _clean(y)


def _sort_starts(lst):
    """Sort list of (score, sequence) by score without lambda."""
    if not lst:
        return []
    scores = np.empty(len(lst), dtype=np.float64)
    for i, item in enumerate(lst):
        s = float(item[0])
        if np.isfinite(s):
            scores[i] = s
        else:
            scores[i] = 1e300
    order = np.argsort(scores)
    out = []
    for i in order:
        out.append(lst[int(i)])
    return out


def _objective_lse(y):
    """
    Smooth surrogate for the exact objective:

        logsumexp(kappa * (x*x))

    with x = softmax(y).
    """
    x = _softmax(y)
    b = _square_convolve(x)
    z = float(_CUR_KAPPA) * b
    m = float(np.max(z)) if z.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return 1e300
    vals = np.exp(z - m)
    return float(m + np.log(float(vals.sum())))


def _objective_lse_grad(y):
    """Gradient of _objective_lse with respect to softmax logits y."""
    x = _softmax(y)
    b = _square_convolve(x)
    z = float(_CUR_KAPPA) * b
    m = float(np.max(z)) if z.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.zeros_like(x)
    w = np.exp(z - m)
    den = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(den) or den <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros_like(x)
    w = w / den

    n = x.size
    # d logsumexp(kappa b) / d x  = 2 kappa * (w * reverse(x))[n-1 : 2n-2]
    c = _convolve_full(w, x[::-1])
    a_part = c[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    A = 2.0 * float(_CUR_KAPPA) * a_part

    s = float(np.dot(x, A))
    grad = x * (A - s)
    return grad


def _run_lbfgs(x0, deadline):
    """
    Run a few L-BFGS passes with increasing log-sum-exp sharpness.
    Returns (best_vector, best_internal_score).
    """
    global _CUR_KAPPA
    x0 = _clean(x0)
    x = x0.copy()
    sx = _score_internal(x)
    n = x.size

    if _scipy_minimize is None or n < 2:
        return x, sx

    if n <= 1024:
        factors = (8.0, 30.0, 100.0)
    elif n <= 4096:
        factors = (12.0, 50.0, 140.0)
    else:
        factors = (20.0, 70.0)

    maxiter = int(max(12, min(120, 130000 // max(3, n))))

    for factor in factors:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
            break

        _CUR_KAPPA = float(n) * float(factor)
        y0 = np.log(x + 1e-300)
        y0 = y0 - float(np.mean(y0))

        try:
            res = _scipy_minimize(
                _objective_lse,
                y0,
                jac=_objective_lse_grad,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "ftol": 1e-10,
                    "gtol": 1e-5,
                    "maxls": 20,
                    "maxcor": 10,
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            continue

        if getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
            continue
        y1 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(y1)):
            continue

        x1 = _softmax(y1)
        s1 = _score_internal(x1)
        if np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < sx - 1e-12:
            x = x1
            sx = s1

    _CUR_KAPPA = 1.0
    return x, sx


def _local_search(x0, deadline, rng):
    """
    Exact-objective simulated-annealing local search on normalized vectors.
    """
    x0 = _clean(x0)
    best = x0.copy()
    cur = best.copy()
    best_score = _score_internal(best)
    cur_score = best_score
    n = best.size

    sigma = 0.10
    temp = 0.025
    stale = 0
    iters = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cand = cur.copy()
        choice = float(rng.random())

        if choice < 0.42:
            # Scale a random block.
            wmin = max(2, n // 200)
            wmax = max(wmin + 1, n // 5)
            wmax = min(n - 1, wmax)
            width = int(rng.integers(wmin, wmax + 1))
            start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width))
            mult = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=width))
            cand[start:start + width] *= mult

        elif choice < 0.66:
            # Add small Gaussian noise to a block.
            wmin = max(1, n // 500)
            wmax = max(wmin + 1, n // 12)
            wmax = min(n - 1, wmax)
            width = int(rng.integers(wmin, wmax + 1))
            start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width))
            scale = max(1e-8, float(np.mean(cur[start:start + width])))
            cand[start:start + width] += sigma * scale * rng.standard_normal(width)

        elif choice < 0.82:
            # Blend two random blocks.
            wmin = max(1, n // 1000)
            wmax = max(wmin + 1, n // 80)
            wmax = min(n - 1, wmax)
            width = int(rng.integers(wmin, wmax + 1))
            p = int(rng.integers(0, n - width))
            q = int(rng.integers(0, n - width))
            if p != q:
                alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.55))
                ap = cand[p:p + width].copy()
                aq = cand[q:q + width].copy()
                cand[p:p + width] = (1.0 - alpha) * ap + alpha * aq
                cand[q:q + width] = (1.0 - alpha) * aq + alpha * ap

        else:
            # Active-coefficient heuristic: move mass away from a strongly
            # contributing pair of one of the largest convolution terms.
            b = _square_convolve(cur)
            if b.size:
                k = int(np.argmax(b))
                lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
                hi = min(n - 1, k)
                if hi >= lo:
                    idx = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int64)
                    weights = cur[idx] * cur[k - idx]
                    pos = int(np.argmax(weights))
                    p = int(lo + pos)
                    q = int(k - p)
                    if 0 <= q < n and p != q:
                        transfer = float(cur[p]) * float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.15))
                        dst = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                        cand[p] = max(0.0, cand[p] - transfer)
                        cand[dst] += transfer
                    else:
                        p = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                        q = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                        if p != q:
                            transfer = float(cur[p]) * float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.10))
                            cand[p] = max(0.0, cand[p] - transfer)
                            cand[q] += transfer
                else:
                    p = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                    q = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                    if p != q:
                        transfer = float(cur[p]) * float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.10))
                        cand[p] = max(0.0, cand[p] - transfer)
                        cand[q] += transfer

        cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
        sc = cand.sum()
        if sc > 1e-300:
            cand = cand / sc
            sc = _score_internal(cand)
        else:
            cand = cur.copy()
            sc = cur_score

        iters += 1

        if np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < cur_score:
                cur = cand
                cur_score = sc
                if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best = cur.copy()
                    best_score = sc
                    stale = 0
                    temp = max(0.0008, temp * 0.97)
                    if best_score < 1.62 or (time.monotonic() - last_print) > 20.0:
                        print(
                            f"[local] iter {iters} best={best_score:.10f} "
                            f"t_left={max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()):.0f}s"
                        )
                        last_print = time.monotonic()
            else:
                accept = False
                delta = sc - cur_score
                if delta < 0.5:
                    prob = float(np.exp(-delta / max(1e-12, temp)))
                    if rng.random() < prob:
                        accept = True
                if accept:
                    cur = cand
                    cur_score = sc
                else:
                    stale += 1
                    if stale % 250 == 0:
                        temp = max(0.0008, temp * 0.90)
                    if stale > 600 and rng.random() < 0.06:
                        mix = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 0.65))
                        cur = (1.0 - mix) * cur + mix * best
                        cur = _clean(cur)
                        cur_score = _score_internal(cur)
                    if stale > 6000:
                        cur = best.copy()
                        cur_score = best_score
                        temp = 0.015
                        stale = 0
        else:
            cur = best.copy()
            cur_score = best_score

    return best, best_score


def _grid_starts(n, rng, deadline, max_candidates):
    """
    Generate a list of starting profiles and evaluate them internally.
    """
    out = []
    max_candidates = int(max_candidates)

    betas = (0.40, 0.44, 0.46, 0.48, 0.49, 0.50, 0.52)
    gammas = (-2.0, -1.0, -0.3, 0.0, 0.3, 1.0, 2.0)
    quads = (-1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0)
    shifts = (0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 3.0)

    for beta in betas:
        for gamma in gammas:
            for quad in quads:
                for shift in shifts:
                    if len(out) >= max_candidates or time.monotonic() > deadline:
                        return out
                    for reverse in (False, True):
                        x = _power_profile(n, beta, gamma, quad, 0.0, shift, reverse)
                        s = _score_internal(x)
                        out.append((s, x))

    # Random refinement of the same family.
    while len(out) < max_candidates and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        beta = float(rng.uniform(0.36, 0.56))
        gamma = float(rng.uniform(-3.0, 3.0))
        quad = float(rng.uniform(-3.0, 3.0))
        cubic = float(rng.uniform(-2.5, 2.5))
        shift = float(rng.choice(_SHIFTS))
        reverse = bool(rng.random() < 0.5)
        x = _power_profile(n, beta, gamma, quad, cubic, shift, reverse)
        s = _score_internal(x)
        out.append((s, x))

    return out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Main entry point.  Returns the best nonnegative sequence found before
    the deadline, aiming to minimize evaluate_sequence().
    """
    start_t = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    budget = max(30.0, budget)
    deadline = start_t + budget - 10.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Try to use the supplied construction when reasonable.
    base = None
    n_base = 0
    if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
        try:
            h = globals()["height_sequence_1"]
            h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if h.size > 0 and h.sum() > 0.0:
                base = h / h.sum()
                n_base = h.size
        except Exception:
            base = None
            n_base = 0

    # Work length: use the supplied length when it is in a practical range,
    # otherwise choose a solid medium length.
    if 800 <= n_base <= 4096:
        n_work = int(n_base)
    else:
        n_work = 2048
    n_work = int(max(512, min(4096, n_work)))

    const = np.ones(n_work, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_work)
    best = const.copy()
    best_score = _score_internal(best)
    print(f"[init] n_work={n_work} constant score={best_score:.10f}")

    starts = []

    # Base starts, if available.
    if base is not None:
        for alpha in (0.0, 0.25, 0.55):
            x = _upsample(base, n_work)
            if alpha > 0.0:
                rx = _random_profile(n_work, rng)
                alpha = min(0.9, float(alpha))
                x = (1.0 - alpha) * x + alpha * rx
            x = _clean(x)
            s = _score_internal(x)
            starts.append((s, x))
            if s < best_score:
                best = x.copy()
                best_score = s
                print(f"[base] score={best_score:.10f}")

    # Grid-based profile generation.  If the work length is large, run the
    # cheaper grid at 2048 and upsample.
    n_profile = n_work if n_work <= 2048 else 2048
    grid_deadline = start_t + 180.0
    if grid_deadline > deadline - 120.0:
        grid_deadline = deadline - 120.0

    if grid_deadline > start_t:
        grid = _grid_starts(n_profile, rng, grid_deadline, max_candidates=320)
        for s, x in grid:
            xw = _upsample(x, n_work)
            sw = _score_internal(xw)
            starts.append((sw, xw))
            if sw < best_score:
                best = xw.copy()
                best_score = sw
                print(f"[grid] n_profile={n_profile} score={best_score:.10f}")
        print(f"[grid] generated {len(grid)} profiles, current best={best_score:.10f}")

    # Random diversity starts.
    for _ in range(12):
        x = _random_profile(n_work, rng)
        s = _score_internal(x)
        starts.append((s, x))
        if s < best_score:
            best = x.copy()
            best_score = s

    # Explicit one-sided half-power endpoints (known strong asymptotic family).
    for shift in (0.0, 0.5, 1.0):
        for rev in (False, True):
            x = _power_profile(n_work, 0.5, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, shift, rev)
            s = _score_internal(x)
            starts.append((s, x))
            if s < best_score:
                best = x.copy()
                best_score = s
                print(f"[power] score={best_score:.10f}")

    starts = _sort_starts(starts)

    # Keep a diverse but compact set of refinement starts.
    top_starts = []
    for score, x in starts:
        if len(top_starts) >= 8:
            break
        if np.isfinite(score):
            top_starts.append(x)
    if not top_starts:
        top_starts.append(best.copy())

    # L-BFGS refinement on the best starting profiles.
    for idx, x0 in enumerate(top_starts):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60.0:
            break
        x0 = _clean(x0)
        s0 = _score_internal(x0)
        print(f"[lbfgs] start {idx} score={s0:.10f}")
        x1, s1 = _run_lbfgs(x0, deadline - 10.0)
        if s1 < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = x1.copy()
            best_score = s1
            print(f"[lbfgs] improved to score={best_score:.10f}")
        elif s1 < s0:
            # Even if not globally best, keep it as a possible local improvement.
            if len(top_starts) < 12:
                top_starts.append(x1.copy())

    # Exact local search with the remaining time.  Reserve time for a possible
    # high-resolution final stage if the score is still not excellent.
    local_deadline = time.monotonic()
    if budget > 1200.0 and best_score > 1.499:
        reserve = min(420.0, max(120.0, (deadline - start_t) * 0.18))
        local_deadline = deadline - reserve
    best, best_score = _local_search(best, max(local_deadline, start_t + 30.0), rng)
    print(f"[local done] score={best_score:.10f} t_left={max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()):.0f}s")

    # Optional high-resolution final stage.  Upscaling a smooth profile often
    # improves finite-length artifacts and can reduce the exact score.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0 and best.size < 8192:
        n_hi = 8192
        x_hi = _upsample(best, n_hi)
        s_hi = _score_internal(x_hi)
        print(f"[upsample] n={n_hi} raw score={s_hi:.10f}")

        if s_hi < best_score - 1e-9:
            best = x_hi.copy()
            best_score = s_hi

        # One shorter L-BFGS pass at the higher resolution, if enough time.
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
            x_hi1, s_hi1 = _run_lbfgs(best, deadline - 60.0)
            if s_hi1 < best_score - 1e-9:
                best = x_hi1.copy()
                best_score = s_hi1
                print(f"[upsample lbfgs] score={best_score:.10f}")

        # Finish with local search at the highest chosen length.
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
            best, best_score = _local_search(best, deadline - 2.0, rng)
            print(f"[upsample local done] score={best_score:.10f}")

    # Final sanitization and return.
    best = _clean(best)
    out = [float(v) for v in best]

    try:
        final_score = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        print(f"[final] length={len(out)} score={final_score:.10f}")
    except Exception as exc:
        print(f"[final] could not verify exact score: {exc}")

    return out
```
